> ## 二维相变
> 
> A variety of 2D phase transitions have been investigated thus far. 迄今已研究了多种二维相变。
> 
> Here, we discuss notable examples, illustrating the richness of phase transformation behaviour in 2D materials. 在此，我们讨论一些显著的例子，以说明二维材料中相变行为的丰富性。
> 
> ### Polymorphic phase transitions
> 
> ### 多晶型相变
> 
> The most prominent examples of 2D materials discovered so far that exhibit polymorphic phase transitions in near-ambient conditions are derived from chalcogenides with layered structures10,101–106. 迄今为止发现的在近环境条件下表现出多晶型相变的最突出的二维材料例子，源自具有层状结构的硫族化物10,101-106。
> 
> **Chalcogenides**: 硫族化物，含有硫、硒或碲等硫族元素的化合物。
> 
> Compared with oxygen and most halogens (F, Cl, Br), chalcogen elements (S, Se and Te) have a relatively small electronegativity and, thus, in many chalcogenides, ionic and covalent bonding compete, which leads to the generation of structural polymorphs with different bonding configurations that are close in energy. 与氧和大多数卤素（F、Cl、Br）相比，硫族元素（S、Se和Te）具有相对较小的电负性，因此，在许多硫族化物中，离子键和共价键相互竞争，导致产生能量相近但具有不同键合构型的结构多晶型。
> 
> **Electronegativity**: 电负性，原子吸引共享电子对的能力的量度。
> 
> Therefore, perturbations induced by weak external stimuli can drive polymorphic transitions in these materials. 因此，弱外部刺激诱导的微扰可以驱动这些材料中的多晶型相变。
> 
> The most widely studied 2D materials exhibiting structural phase transitions are group 6 transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers with the chemical formula MX2, where M stands for Mo or W and X stands for S, Se or Te. 研究最广泛的具有结构相变的二维材料是第6族过渡金属二硫族化物单层，化学式为MX₂，其中M代表Mo或W，X代表S、Se或Te。
> 
> Monolayer MX2 materials have three well-known structural polymorphs46,107, that is, 2H, 1T and 1T′ (Fig. 3a). 单层MX₂材料有三种众所周知的结构多晶型46,107，即2H、1T和1T′（图3a）。
> 
> The 2H phase has a trigonal prismatic coordination pattern, whereas the coordination patterns in the 1T and 1T′ phases are octahedral and distorted octahedral, respectively. 2H相具有三角棱柱配位模式，而1T和1T′相中的配位模式分别为八面体和畸变八面体。
> 
> Monolayer MX2 in the semiconducting 2H phase has an optical bandgap in the range of 1.0–2.5 eV (reF.108). 半导体2H相的单层MX₂具有1.0–2.5 eV范围内的光学带隙（参考文献108）。
> 
> **Optical bandgap**: 光学带隙，电子从价带跃迁到导带所需的最小光子能量。
> 
> The 1T phase is metallic but unstable in isolation and subject to Peierls distortion48,49. 1T相是金属性的，但在孤立状态下不稳定，会发生派尔斯畸变48,49。
> 
> The 1T phase spontaneously transforms into the 1T′ phase through the distortion of octahedral coordination. 1T相通过八面体配位的畸变自发转变为1T′相。
> 
> The 1T′ phase of monolayer MX2 was first theoretically predicted33 and then experimentally confirmed34,35,109 to be a QSH insulator with a sizeable bandgap on the order of 0.1 eV (reFs33,49,110). 单层MX₂的1T′相首先被理论预测33，随后被实验证实34,35,109为具有约0.1 eV量级可观测带隙的QSH绝缘体（参考文献33,49,110）。
> 
> The 1 × 2 Peierls reconstruction causes the metal-dominated d-band, which is typically the electron donor, to become lower in energy than the chalcogen-dominated p-band around the Γ-point, causing a topological band inversion and 'tying the knot' in the electronic band structure in the Brillouin zone. 1×2派尔斯重构导致通常作为电子施主的金属主导d带在Γ点附近比硫族元素主导的p带能量更低，引起拓扑能带反转并在布里渊区的电子能带结构中"打结"。
> 
> **Brillouin zone**: 布里渊区，倒空间中晶格周期性的基本单元，电子能带结构在其中定义。
> 
> This gives a perfect edge conductance of e2/h = 1/25.813 kΩ at T < 100 K for 1T′-WTe2 monolayer35 with magnetic field dependence, and, combined with the fact that it is also a 2D superconductor at Tc ≈ 0.7 K (reF.111), gives rise to the prospect of using it for topological quantum computing. 这赋予了1T′-WTe₂单层在T < 100 K时具有磁场依赖性的完美边缘电导e²/h = 1/25.813 kΩ 35，并且结合它也是在Tc ≈ 0.7 K时的二维超导体（参考文献111）这一事实，为其用于拓扑量子计算带来了前景。
> 
> **Topological quantum computing**: 拓扑量子计算，利用拓扑量子态进行量子信息处理的方法，具有对局部噪声的固有容错能力。
> 
> Theoretical calculations based on density functional theory (DFT) indicate that the 2H phase is the ground-state phase of all monolayer MX2, except for WTe2, for which the 1T′ phase has the lowest energy48 (Fig. 3b). 基于密度泛函理论（DFT）的理论计算表明，2H相是所有单层MX₂的基态相，但WTe₂除外，其1T′相具有最低能量48（图3b）。
> 
> **Density functional theory (DFT)**: 密度泛函理论，一种基于电子密度而非多体波函数来求解多电子体系电子结构的量子力学计算方法。
> 
> The energy differences between the 2H and 1T′ phases decrease in the order of MS2→MSe2→MTe2. 2H和1T′相之间的能量差按MS₂→MSe₂→MTe₂的顺序递减。
> 
> For MoS2, the DFT-computed energy of the 1T′ phase is ~0.5 eV per formula unit higher than that of the 2H phase, whereas for MoTe2, the DFT-computed energy is only 43 meV per formula unit higher. 对于MoS₂，DFT计算的1T′相能量比2H相高约0.5 eV/化学式单位，而对于MoTe₂，DFT计算的能量仅高43 meV/化学式单位。
> 
> Thus, chemical processes are required for the transition between the 2H and 1T′ phases of MoS2. 因此，MoS₂的2H和1T′相之间的转变需要通过化学过程来实现。
> 
> By contrast, for MoTe2, the H-to-T′ transition can be induced by non-chemical factors, including temperature49,112, tensile strain48,89,90, laser irradiation50, electrostatic doping51,113 and electric fields52. 相比之下，对于MoTe₂，H到T′的转变可由非化学因素诱导，包括温度49,112、拉伸应变48,89,90、激光辐照50、静电掺杂51,113和电场52。
> 
> #### Chemically induced transition.
> 
> #### 化学诱导的相变
> 
> The transition of MoS2 from the 2H phase to the 1T or 1T′ phase occurs through the intercalation of alkali metals107,114,115 by charge transfer from the s orbitals of alkali metals to the d orbitals of the transition-metal atoms. MoS₂从2H相到1T或1T′相的转变通过碱金属的插层107,114,115发生，通过从碱金属s轨道到过渡金属原子d轨道的电荷转移实现。
> 
> **Intercalation**: 插层，将原子、离子或分子可逆地插入到层状材料层间的过程。
> 
> The increase in d-orbital filling causes the stability of the 1T or 1T′ phase to be higher than that of the 2H phase beyond a certain doping threshold. d轨道填充的增加导致在超过一定掺杂阈值后，1T或1T′相的稳定性高于2H相。
> 
> This phenomenon can be understood in terms of crystal field theory10,101,116. 这一现象可以从晶体场理论10,101,116的角度来理解。
> 
> **Crystal field theory**: 晶体场理论，描述过渡金属离子中d轨道在周围配体电场作用下能级分裂的理论。
> 
> An incomplete doping-induced phase transition in monolayer MoS2 can give rise to the coexistence of 2H, 1T and 1T′ phases with coherent phase boundaries46. 单层MoS₂中不完全的掺杂诱导相变可导致2H、1T和1T′相以共格相界共存46。
> 
> The 2H phase of MoS2 is semiconducting, whereas the 1T phase is metallic, motivating the patterning of metallic and semiconducting regions on monolayer MoS2 by area-selective phase transformation47 (Fig. 3c). MoS₂的2H相是半导体的，而1T相是金属性的，这推动了通过区域选择性相变47在单层MoS₂上图案化金属和半导体区域（图3c）。
> 
> The metallic 1T region can serve as the low-resistance contact for MoS2 transistors, leading to improved device performance, including high drive currents, high electron mobility, low subthreshold swing values and large on/off ratios47. 金属性1T区域可作为MoS₂晶体管的低电阻接触，从而改善器件性能，包括高驱动电流、高电子迁移率、低亚阈值摆幅值和大的开关比47。
> 
> **Subthreshold swing**: 亚阈值摆幅，场效应晶体管中电流增加一个数量级所需的栅极电压变化，反映开关效率。
> 
> #### Thermally driven transition.
> 
> #### 热驱动相变
> 
> Compared with MoS2, the 2H and 1T′ phases of MoTe2 have a smaller energy difference, enabling a wider range of external stimuli to induce the 2H-to-1T′ phase transition. 与MoS₂相比，MoTe₂的2H和1T′相具有较小的能量差，使得更广泛的外部刺激能够诱导2H到1T′的相变。
> 
> Temperature alone can drive the phase transition of MoTe2 from the 2H phase to the 1T′ phase, owing to the higher vibrational and electronic entropy of the 1T′ phase as compared with the 2H phase49,112. 仅温度就可以驱动MoTe₂从2H相到1T′相的相变，因为1T′相与2H相相比具有更高的振动熵和电子熵49,112。
> 
> The transition temperature in bulk MoTe2, which depends on the deficiency or excess of Te, is 500–820 °C, with Te deficiencies causing lower transition temperatures49 (Fig. 3d). 块体MoTe₂中的转变温度取决于Te的缺乏或过量，为500–820°C，Te缺乏导致较低的转变温度49（图3d）。
> 
> Theoretical calculations indicate that the transition temperature difference between bulk and monolayer MoTe2 is modest (<20%), owing to weak phonon dispersion along the out-of-plane direction112. 理论计算表明，由于沿面外方向的弱声子色散，块体和单层MoTe₂之间的转变温度差异不大（<20%）112。
> 
> **Phonon dispersion**: 声子色散，声子频率随波矢的变化关系。
> 
> By alloying MoTe2 with WTe2, the 2H-to-1T′ phase transition temperature can be substantially decreased112, because the 1T′ phase is the stable phase of WTe2 at ambient conditions. 通过将MoTe₂与WTe₂合金化，可以大幅降低2H到1T′的相变温度112，因为1T′相是WTe₂在环境条件下的稳定相。
> 
> #### Strain-driven transition.
> 
> #### 应变驱动相变
> 
> Tensile-strain-induced transitions between the 2H and 1T′ phases of MoTe2 and other MX2 monolayers have been theoretically48 and experimentally investigated89,90. MoTe₂和其他MX₂单层的2H和1T′相之间的拉伸应变诱导相变已在理论上48和实验上89,90得到研究。
> 
> The driving force for strain-induced phase transitions originates from the different unit-cell dimensions of the 2H and 1T′ phases, allowing the system to minimize the thermodynamic potential by transforming into a phase that reduces the elastic strain energy. 应变诱导相变的驱动力源于2H和1T′相的不同晶胞尺寸，使得系统可以通过转变为减小弹性应变能的相来最小化热力学势。
> 
> The critical strain value needed to drive the 2H-to-1T′ phase transition is expected to depend on the mechanical boundary conditions48. 驱动2H到1T′相变所需的临界应变值预期取决于力学边界条件48。
> 
> Experimentally, a phase transition in MoTe2 thin films has been demonstrated at room temperature with a 0.2% in-plane biaxial strain89. 实验上，MoTe₂薄膜中的相变已在室温下以0.2%的面内双轴应变得到证实89。
> 
> This level of strain can be generated by mechanical deformation of a suspended 2D material using an AFM tip89 or through the inverse piezoelectric effect of a ferroelectric, to which the 2D material is clamped90 (Fig. 3e). 这种水平的应变可以通过使用AFM针尖89对悬浮二维材料进行机械变形，或通过二维材料所夹持的铁电体的逆压电效应90产生（图3e）。
> 
> **Inverse piezoelectric effect**: 逆压电效应，施加电场导致压电材料发生机械变形的现象。
> 
> #### Laser-driven transition.
> 
> #### 激光驱动相变
> 
> Laser irradiation can cause the irreversible transition of a multilayer MoTe2 in the 2H phase to the 1T′ phase (Fig. 3f), owing to the generation of Te vacancies50. 激光辐照可导致多层MoTe₂从2H相不可逆地转变为1T′相（图3f），这是由于Te空位的产生50。
> 
> DFT calculations showed that the formation of 3% Te vacancies alters the relative stability between the 2H and 1T′ phases. DFT计算表明，3% Te空位的形成改变了2H和1T′相之间的相对稳定性。
> 
> This mechanism is consistent with the reduced 2H-to-1T′ thermal phase transition temperature in MoTe2 with a Te deficiency49. 这一机制与Te缺乏的MoTe₂中2H到1T′热相变温度降低一致49。
> 
> Laser-induced phase transition enables the local patterning of metallic 1T′ regions in 2H-MoTe2 multilayers, akin to phase patterning using chemical methods in MoS2 (reF.47). 激光诱导相变能够在2H-MoTe₂多层中局部图案化金属性1T′区域，类似于在MoS₂中使用化学方法的相图案化（参考文献47）。
> 
> The metallic 1T′ phase forms an ohmic homojunction with the semiconducting 2H phase, substantially increasing the carrier mobility of MoTe2 transistors. 金属性1T′相与半导体2H相形成欧姆同质结，显著提高了MoTe₂晶体管的载流子迁移率。
> 
> **Ohmic homojunction**: 欧姆同质结，相同材料但不同相之间形成的低电阻接触界面。
> 
> #### Electrostatic-doping-driven transition.
> 
> #### 静电掺杂驱动相变
> 
> Electrostatic doping through the application of a gate voltage can generate a carrier density of up to 1014 e cm−2 in monolayer MX2 (reF.68), corresponding to ~0.1 e per formula unit. 通过施加栅极电压的静电掺杂可在单层MX₂中产生高达10¹⁴ e cm⁻²的载流子密度（参考文献68），相当于约0.1 e每化学式单位。
> 
> This feature has motivated the study of electrostatic-doping-induced structural phase transitions in MX2 monolayers. 这一特征推动了对MX₂单层中静电掺杂诱导结构相变的研究。
> 
> In an electrostatic gating device configuration, in which an appropriate dielectric is sandwiched between a MX2 monolayer and a metal plate, a gate voltage of several volts can drive a 2H-to-1T′ phase transition of monolayer MoTe2, as shown by DFT-based theoretical analysis80,113. 在静电栅控器件构型中，适当的介电层夹在MX₂单层和金属板之间，几伏的栅极电压即可驱动单层MoTe₂的2H到1T′相变，如基于DFT的理论分析所示80,113。
> 
> Electrostatic-doping-induced phase transition was further experimentally demonstrated in monolayer MoTe2 using ionic liquid as the dielectric in a field-effect-transistor configuration51 (Fig. 3g); a gate voltage of ~4 V fully converted the 2H phase into the 1T′ phase. 静电掺杂诱导的相变进一步在单层MoTe₂中通过使用离子液体作为场效应晶体管构型中的介电层得到实验证实51（图3g）；约4 V的栅极电压将2H相完全转换为1T′相。
> 
> **Ionic liquid**: 离子液体，完全由离子组成的液态盐，具有高电化学稳定性和大电容，常用于静电掺杂。
> 
> #### Electric-field-driven transition.
> 
> #### 电场驱动相变
> 
> In vertical devices based on MoTe2 and Mo1−xWxTe2 thin films, an electric field applied across the film thickness direction can induce the transition of MX2 from the 2H phase into a distorted transient structure, 2Hd, in which the monolayer structure is distinct from the three known phases of MX2 (reF.52) (Fig. 3h). 在基于MoTe₂和Mo₁₋ₓWₓTe₂薄膜的垂直器件中，施加在薄膜厚度方向上的电场可诱导MX₂从2H相转变为畸变的瞬态结构2Hd，其中单层结构不同于MX₂的三种已知相（参考文献52）（图3h）。
> 
> The exact atomic configuration of monolayers in the 2Hd phase has not yet been fully resolved; however, experimental evidence52 suggests that 2Hd monolayers have a transitional structure, between 2H and 1T′. 2Hd相中单层的精确原子构型尚未完全解析；然而，实验证据52表明，2Hd单层具有介于2H和1T′之间的过渡结构。
> 
> As a side remark, a strong enough vertical electric field can also cause topological-insulator to normal-insulator transition within the same structural polymorph33, an extremely rapid (femtosecond level) transition in the quantum Z2 invariant for the electronic structure. 作为旁注，足够强的垂直电场也可在同一结构多晶型内引起拓扑绝缘体到正常绝缘体的转变33，这是电子结构量子Z₂不变量的极快（飞秒级）转变。
> 
> **Z2 invariant**: Z₂不变量，描述时间反演不变拓扑绝缘体拓扑性质的量子数，取值为0（普通绝缘体）或1（拓扑绝缘体）。
> 
> ### Ferroic phase transitions
> 
> ### 铁性相变
> 
> Ferroics possess two or more orientation states (domain variants), which are degenerate in free energy and can be switched by an external field117. 铁性材料具有两个或更多取向态（畴变体），它们在自由能上简并，并可通过外场切换117。
> 
> **Ferroics**: 铁性材料，具有可被外场切换的简并取向态的材料总称。
> 
> Ferroics can be categorized into four types, based on the properties associated with the orientation states, that is, ferroelectrics, ferromagnets, ferroelastics and ferrotoroidics. 铁性材料可根据与取向态相关的性质分为四类，即铁电体、铁磁体、铁弹体和铁涡体。
> 
> **Ferroelectrics**: 铁电体，具有可被外电场反转的自发极化的材料。 **Ferromagnets**: 铁磁体，具有可被外磁场反转的自发磁化的材料。 **Ferroelastics**: 铁弹体，具有可被外应力反转的自发应变的材料。 **Ferrotoroidics**: 铁涡体，具有可被外场反转的自发涡旋有序的材料。
> 
> Ferroics have been extensively studied in bulk form. 铁性材料已在块体形式中得到广泛研究。
> 
> Ferroelectrics show spontaneous electric polarization, which can be switched by an electric field; ferromagnets show spontaneous magnetization, which is switchable by a magnetic field; ferroelastics possess domain variants with spontaneous stress-free strain, which can be switched by applying stress; and ferrotoroidics possess internal chiral order. 铁电体显示出可被电场切换的自发电极化；铁磁体显示出可被磁场切换的自发磁化；铁弹体具有可被施加应力切换的具有自发无应力应变的畴变体；铁涡体具有内部手性有序。
> 
> **Spontaneous polarization**: 自发极化，在没有外电场情况下材料内部存在的电极化。
> 
> Ferroic materials are typically formed through ferroic phase transitions characterized by the loss of point-group symmetry operations, for example, by spatial inversion in the case of ferroelectricity, rotational symmetry in the case of ferroelasticity or time-reversal symmetry in the case of ferromagnetism117. 铁性材料通常通过铁性相变形成，其特征是点群对称操作的丧失，例如，铁电性的空间反演、铁弹性的旋转对称性或铁磁性的时间反演对称性117。
> 
> **Point-group symmetry**: 点群对称性，描述晶体宏观对称性的所有对称操作集合。
> 
> A material is multiferroic if more than one ferroic order is present. 如果材料中存在多于一种铁性序，则该材料为多铁性材料。
> 
> **Multiferroic**: 多铁性，同时具有两种或以上铁性序（如铁电性和铁磁性）的材料。
> 
> #### Ferroelastic transition.
> 
> #### 铁弹相变
> 
> The high elastic strain limit and the abundance of structures with low symmetry render 2D materials promising candidates for distinct mechanical responses related to ferroelasticity. 高弹性应变极限和丰富的低对称性结构使二维材料成为与铁弹性相关的独特机械响应的有前途的候选者。
> 
> In group 6 transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers, the 1T-to-1T′ phase transition was theoretically predicted to be a ferroelastic phase transition accompanied by spontaneous hydrostatic and shear strains57, which has been supported experimentally118. 在第6族过渡金属二硫族化物单层中，1T到1T′的相变在理论上被预测为伴随自发静水应变和剪切应变的铁弹相变57，并已得到实验支持118。
> 
> The three symmetry-equivalent directions of structural distortion lead to three orientation variants with distinct unit-cell dimensions. 结构畸变的三个对称等价方向导致三个具有不同晶胞尺寸的取向变体。
> 
> Thus, transformation strains (also called stress-free strains) of a variant can couple to the energetics of the system, causing the transformation from one orientation variant to another. 因此，一个变体的相变应变（也称为无应力应变）可与系统的能量学耦合，导致从一个取向变体到另一个的转变。
> 
> DFT calculations suggested that a tensile strain of a few percent is sufficient to switch monolayer 1T′-WTe2 from one orientation domain to another. DFT计算表明，百分之几的拉伸应变足以将单层1T′-WTe₂从一个取向畴切换到另一个。
> 
> Strain energy minimization can also lead to the coexistence of different domains and the stable formation of domain or phase boundaries (Fig. 4a). 应变能最小化也可导致不同畴的共存以及畴界或相界的稳定形成（图4a）。
> 
> Monolayer 1T′-MX2 are QSH insulators33 and, therefore, their phase boundaries possess distinct electronic transport properties, owing to the bulk-edge correspondence in topologically non-trivial systems119,120. 单层1T′-MX₂是QSH绝缘体33，因此，由于拓扑非平凡体系中的体-边对应关系，它们的相界具有独特的电子输运性质119,120。
> 
> **Bulk-edge correspondence**: 体-边对应关系，拓扑绝缘体中体态拓扑性质必然导致边界上存在导电态的物理原理。
> 
> First-principles calculations have also shown that α-SnO becomes ferroelastic if the thickness of the material is at the monolayer level121. 第一性原理计算还表明，如果材料厚度在单层级别，α-SnO会变成铁弹体121。
> 
> **First-principles calculations**: 第一性原理计算，从量子力学基本方程出发，不依赖经验参数的理论计算方法。
> 
> The low-temperature ferroelastic phase of α-SnO has an orthorhombic symmetry with two equivalent orientation states. α-SnO的低温铁弹相具有正交对称性，有两个等价的取向态。
> 
> The degenerate structural ground states can be energetically biased when illuminated with a linearly polarized laser pulse59. 简并的结构基态在用线偏振激光脉冲照射时可在能量上发生偏置59。
> 
> For example, when the system is subjected to an alternating electric field with a particular polarization direction, the anisotropic dielectric response of the low-symmetry ferroelastic phase will lead to a non-degenerate free energy landscape along the reaction coordinate of the phase transformation, which is similar to optical tweezing of dielectric microbeads, but along the generalized reaction coordinate of the phase transformation instead of the x–y position of a bead. 例如，当系统受到具有特定偏振方向的交变电场作用时，低对称性铁弹相的各向异性介电响应将导致沿相变反应坐标的非简并自由能景观，这类似于介电微珠的光镊，但沿相变的广义反应坐标而非微珠的x-y位置。
> 
> **Optical tweezing**: 光镊，利用聚焦激光束捕获和操控微观粒子的技术。
> 
> Phase-change materials have been proposed to operate on the basis of such optomechanically driven 2D martensitic transitions59,60, which have multiple advantages compared with traditional, thermally driven phase-change systems (such as Ge-Sb-Te alloys), including faster transformation kinetics, reduced energy dissipation and more robust resistance against functional fatigue. 相变材料已被提议基于此类光机械驱动的二维马氏体相变59,60运行，与传统的热驱动相变系统（如Ge-Sb-Te合金）相比，它具有多种优势，包括更快的相变动力学、更少的能量耗散以及更强的抗功能疲劳性。
> 
> **Martensitic transitions**: 马氏体相变，原子通过协调的切变而非扩散方式完成的无扩散、位移型结构相变。
> 
> #### Ferroelectric transition.
> 
> #### 铁电相变
> 
> Ferroelectricity in thin-film perovskite materials has been intensively studied122; however, in ferroelectrics with out-of-plane polarization, the ferroelectric order disappears if the film thickness is below a critical value, owing to the effects of the depolarization field and surface reconstruction122,123. 薄膜钙钛矿材料中的铁电性已被深入研究122；然而，在具有面外极化的铁电体中，由于退极化场和表面重构的影响，如果薄膜厚度低于临界值，铁电序会消失122,123。
> 
> **Depolarization field**: 退极化场，铁电体中束缚电荷产生的与自发极化方向相反的电场，会削弱铁电性。
> 
> By contrast, in-plane polarization is less susceptible to the thickness-dependent depolarization effect, and, thus, ferroelectric order may remain stable in 2D. 相比之下，面内极化对厚度依赖的退极化效应不那么敏感，因此，铁电序在二维中可能保持稳定。
> 
> Indeed, robust in-plane ferroelectricity has been experimentally realized in atomic-thick SnTe, with a ferroelectric transition temperature of 270 K (reF.55) (Fig. 4b). 事实上，已在原子级厚度的SnTe中实验实现了稳健的面内铁电性，铁电转变温度为270 K（参考文献55）（图4b）。
> 
> Ferroic phase transitions have also been theoretically investigated in group IV monochalcogenide MX (M= Ge, Sn; X = S, Se), which are closely related to SnTe (reFs58,124–128). 铁性相变也在第IV族单硫族化物MX（M = Ge, Sn; X = S, Se）中得到了理论研究，它们与SnTe密切相关（参考文献58,124-128）。
> 
> MX monolayers can further be ferroelectric and ferroelastic at the same time, making them multiferroic58,124. MX单层还可以同时具有铁电性和铁弹性，使其成为多铁性材料58,124。
> 
> Ferroelectricity has also been experimentally demonstrated in other 2D systems, including In2Se3 (reFs56,129–132), CuInP2S6 (reFs133,134), monolayer MoTe2 with distorted 1T phase135,136, as well as bilayers and trilayers of WTe2 (reF.70). 铁电性也在其他二维系统中得到了实验证实，包括In₂Se₃（参考文献56,129-132）、CuInP₂S₆（参考文献133,134）、具有畸变1T相的单层MoTe₂ 135,136，以及WTe₂的双层和三层（参考文献70）。
> 
> Notably, 2D α-In2Se3 possesses both out-of-plane and in-plane ferroelectric orders, and the out-of-plane polarization is resistant to the depolarization field owing to the locking between out-of-plane dipoles and in-plane lattice asymmetry56,131. 值得注意的是，二维α-In₂Se₃同时具有面外和面内铁电序，并且由于面外偶极子与面内晶格不对称性之间的锁定，面外极化对退极化场具有抵抗力56,131。
> 
> Furthermore, out-of-plane ferroelectricity can be present in 2D semimetals, for example, in few-layer WTe2, because dielectric screening in the layer-normal direction is suppressed in a 2D system. 此外，面外铁电性可存在于二维半金属中，例如在少层WTe₂中，因为二维系统中层法线方向的介电屏蔽被抑制。
> 
> **Semimetal**: 半金属，导带和价带有微小重叠或恰好接触的材料，具有有限载流子浓度但态密度较低。
> 
> Remarkably, ultrafast symmetry switching behaviour was observed in 3D bulk WTe2 (reF.53), a type II Weyl semimetal137. 值得注意的是，在三维块体WTe₂（参考文献53）——一种第二类外尔半金属137——中观察到了超快对称性切换行为。
> 
> **Weyl semimetal**: 外尔半金属，能带相交形成外尔点的拓扑半金属，具有手性电荷和表面费米弧。
> 
> In contrast with monolayer 1T′-WTe2, which is centrosymmetric, multilayer and bulk WTe2 have an orthorhombic structure with distorted octahedral coordination and broken inversion symmetry (the Td phase, space group Pmn21)138. 与中心对称的单层1T′-WTe₂不同，多层和块体WTe₂具有正交结构，具有畸变八面体配位和破缺的反演对称性（Td相，空间群Pmn2₁）138。
> 
> **Centrosymmetric**: 中心对称的，晶体具有反演中心作为对称操作。
> 
> Upon electric gating along the layer-normal direction, multilayer WTe2 shows ferroelectric switching with a butterfly-shaped conductance curve54,70. 沿层法线方向施加电栅控时，多层WTe₂显示出具有蝴蝶形电导曲线的铁电切换54,70。
> 
> Different from local atomic-movement-induced ferroelectric transition139 (for example, through breaking and switching of local W–W bonds or by local distortion and rotation of W–Te bonds), low-energy interlayer gliding is likely responsible for the switching behaviour in multilayer and bulk WTe2 (reFs71,140). 与局部原子运动诱导的铁电转变139（例如，通过局部W-W键的断裂和切换或通过W-Te键的局部畸变和旋转）不同，低能层间滑移很可能是多层和块体WTe₂中切换行为的原因（参考文献71,140）。
> 
> #### Ferromagnetic transition.
> 
> #### 铁磁相变
> 
> A consequence of the Hohenberg–Mermin–Wagner theorem12,13 is that long-range ferromagnetism cannot exist at finite temperature in 2D, if the spin Hamiltonian has a continuous symmetry with sufficient short-range interactions13, as ample gapless spin waves will destroy the ferromagnetic state. 霍恩贝格-默明-瓦格纳定理12,13的一个推论是，如果自旋哈密顿量具有连续对称性和足够的短程相互作用，则长程铁磁性在二维中无法在有限温度下存在13，因为大量无能隙的自旋波将破坏铁磁态。
> 
> **Spin waves**: 自旋波，磁有序系统中自旋偏离有序排列的集体激发，量子化后称为磁振子。
> 
> However, ferromagnetism can be stabilized at finite temperature if magnetic anisotropy is present in the system141–143. 然而，如果系统中存在磁各向异性，铁磁性可在有限温度下稳定存在141-143。
> 
> **Magnetic anisotropy**: 磁各向异性，材料磁性质随方向变化的现象，可产生能隙而抑制自旋波涨落。
> 
> Furthermore, finite size effects can stabilize phase transitions in 2D, including a ferromagnetic transition, because the divergence of fluctuations in 2D is only logarithmic with sample size and, thus, a slow divergence62. 此外，有限尺寸效应可以稳定二维中的相变，包括铁磁相变，因为二维中涨落的发散仅与样品尺寸成对数关系，因此是一种缓慢的发散62。
> 
> Ferromagnetism has been experimentally observed in a range of 2D materials41–45, including semiconducting Cr2Ge2Te6 (reF.40), CrI3 (reF.39), CrXBr (X = S, Se)144–147 and metallic Fe3GeTe2 (reFs70,148,149) (Fig. 4c,d). 铁磁性已在一系列二维材料中被实验观察到41-45，包括半导体Cr₂Ge₂Te₆（参考文献40）、CrI₃（参考文献39）、CrXBr（X = S, Se）144-147和金属Fe₃GeTe₂（参考文献70,148,149）（图4c,d）。
> 
> ### High-temperature diffusive phase changes
> 
> ### 高温扩散型相变
> 
> In diffusive transformations, changes in labelled atomic registries are non-deterministic, and the macroscopic order parameters of the new phase or domain do not reveal detailed atom-to-atom correspondences between the old and new phases, because changes in the registry are caused by multiple randomized atomic hops. 在扩散型相变中，被标记原子的位置变化是非确定性的，新相或畴的宏观序参量不揭示新旧相之间详细的原子对应关系，因为位置变化是由多次随机原子跳跃引起的。
> 
> **Diffusive transformations**: 扩散型相变，原子通过长程随机扩散完成重排的相变，原子位移是非确定性的。
> 
> By contrast, in an ideal displacive transformation, the nearest-neighbour relation of an atom either remains unchanged or changes in a deterministic way within a domain variant. 相比之下，在理想的位移型相变中，原子的最近邻关系要么保持不变，要么在畴变体内以确定性的方式变化。
> 
> Displacive transformations are often driven by a strong directional field, for example, stress, an electric field or a magnetic field. 位移型相变通常由强方向场驱动，例如应力、电场或磁场。
> 
> Most ferroelastic and ferroelectric transitions, which involve the loss of rotational symmetry or spatial inversion symmetry, are displacive transformations. 大多数铁弹和铁电相变涉及旋转对称性或空间反演对称性的丧失，属于位移型相变。
> 
> The kinetics of displacive transformations are characterized by a threshold-type behaviour of the domain-growth speed v with respect to the thermodynamic driving force F; the nonlinear v(F) is nearly zero below a certain threshold, but substantially increases above a threshold Fthresh, and then saturates at the phonon group velocity. 位移型相变的动力学特征是畴生长速度v相对于热力学驱动力F的阈值类型行为；非线性的v(F)在某一阈值以下几乎为零，但在阈值Fthresh以上显著增加，然后在声子群速度处饱和。
> 
> **Phonon group velocity**: 声子群速度，声子波包能量传播的速度，是晶体中声速的上限。
> 
> The growth speed of a displacive phase-change domain thus has to be fitted by a highly nonlinear function v ∝ (F/Fthresh)n, where n can be as large as 20. 因此，位移型相变畴的生长速度必须用高度非线性的函数v ∝ (F/Fthresh)ⁿ来描述，其中n可高达20。
> 
> In comparison, diffusive transformations have a more linear-response-type growth velocity law v(F), where n is 1 or close to 1, but is often much more temperature-sensitive. 相比之下，扩散型相变具有更接近线性响应类型的生长速度律v(F)，其中n为1或接近1，但通常对温度更加敏感。
> 
> Diffusive motion is usually thermally activated and, therefore, more prevalent at high temperatures, at which atomic defects have higher concentration and also become more labile, allowing the system to fully sample the configurational space (entropy), which results in an optimal organization (phase) and, thus, approaching the equilibrium phase diagram. 扩散运动通常是热激活的，因此，在高温下更普遍，在此温度下，原子缺陷浓度更高，也更加不稳定，使得系统能够充分遍历构型空间（熵），从而产生最优组织（相），并因此接近平衡相图。
> 
> **Thermally activated**: 热激活的，过程需要克服由热涨落提供的能垒才能发生。
> 
> By changing the chemical precursors during chemical vapour deposition, 2D [Mo,W]S2 forms either a two-phase MoS2/WS2 heterostructure with sharp phase boundaries at 650 °C or a single-phase random solid-solution MoxW1−xS2 at ≥680 °C (reF.150) (Fig. 5a). 通过改变化学气相沉积过程中的化学前驱体，二维[Mo,W]S₂在650°C时形成具有尖锐相界的MoS₂/WS₂两相异质结构，或在≥680°C时形成单相随机固溶体MoₓW₁₋ₓS₂（参考文献150）（图5a）。
> 
> **Chemical vapour deposition**: 化学气相沉积，通过气相前驱体在基底上反应生成固体薄膜的工艺。 **Solid-solution**: 固溶体，一种组成元素以原子尺度混合在另一种元素晶格中的均匀固相。
> 
> Compared with lattice diffusion in 3D crystals, diffusion in 2D crystals is easier because surface diffusivity is commonly acknowledged to be much faster than bulk diffusivity. 与三维晶体中的晶格扩散相比，二维晶体中的扩散更容易，因为普遍认为表面扩散系数远快于块体扩散系数。
> 
> The phase-separated MoS2/WS2 heterostructure can be applied in optoelectronics and the solid-solution MoxW1−xS2 alloy phase can be used to tune the relative phase stabilities of different polymorphs (2H, 1T and 1T′) by changing the Mo/W ratio. 相分离的MoS₂/WS₂异质结构可应用于光电子学，而固溶体MoₓW₁₋ₓS₂合金相可通过改变Mo/W比例来调控不同多晶型（2H、1T和1T′）的相对相稳定性。
> 
> Generally speaking, at high temperatures, it is important to probe and precisely navigate the equilibrium temperature-composition phase diagram. 一般而言，在高温下，探测并精确导航平衡温度-成分相图非常重要。
> 
> **Phase diagram**: 相图，表示材料在不同温度、压力、成分等条件下稳定相状态的图表。
> 
> The size, morphology and defect content of 2D materials not only depend on the composition but also on the growth conditions (Fig. 5b). 二维材料的尺寸、形貌和缺陷含量不仅取决于成分，还取决于生长条件（图5b）。
> 
> For example, large-area growth of 2D metal chalcogenides can be achieved by using molten salt as a growth medium151, which reduces the melting point of metal-ion-bearing precursors and allows passivation of different growth edges152. 例如，二维金属硫族化物的大面积生长可通过使用熔盐作为生长介质151来实现，这降低了含金属离子前驱体的熔点，并允许钝化不同的生长边缘152。
> 
> **Molten salt**: 熔盐，在高温下呈液态的盐，可用作晶体生长的溶剂介质。
> 
> Moreover, under out-of-thermal-equilibrium conditions, for example, under external irradiation (photons, electrons), electrical current or plasma treatment, phase changes can occur in a manner that does not abide by the equilibrium phase diagram153. 此外，在热非平衡条件下，例如在外部辐照（光子、电子）、电流或等离子体处理下，相变可以以不遵循平衡相图的方式发生153。
> 
> In addition, conditions can be designed to directly grow thermodynamically metastable phases105,154,155 (Fig. 5c). 此外，可以设计条件以直接生长热力学亚稳相105,154,155（图5c）。
> 
> **Metastable phases**: 亚稳相，在给定条件下不是热力学最稳定相，但由于动力学限制而能长期存在的相。
> 
> 2D and 2D-templated diffusive phase transformations have played a key role in the development of phase-change memory (PCM) materials. 二维和以二维为模板的扩散型相变在相变存储器（PCM）材料的发展中发挥了关键作用。
> 
> **Phase-change memory (PCM)**: 相变存储器，利用材料在晶态和非晶态之间快速可逆相变来实现数据存储的技术。
> 
> PCM provided the basis for information storage in CDs and DVDs, as well as for the newest generation of non-volatile solid-state drives. PCM为CD和DVD中的信息存储以及最新一代非易失性固态硬盘提供了基础。
> 
> **Non-volatile**: 非易失性，存储器在断电后仍能保持数据的能力。
> 
> For example, sub-nanosecond writing has been achieved with Sc0.2Sb2Te3 as the active crystalline-to-amorphous phase-change material156. 例如，使用Sc₀.₂Sb₂Te₃作为活性晶态-非晶态相变材料，已实现了亚纳秒级写入156。
> 
> However, several challenges remain to be addressed, including high-temperature-induced volatility and resistance-level drifting157, which will require a thorough understanding and fine control of the ionic motion during device operation. 然而，仍有一些挑战有待解决，包括高温诱导的挥发性问题和电阻水平漂移157，这需要透彻理解并精细控制器件操作过程中的离子运动。
> 
> Owing to their atomic thickness, 2D active materials have distinct thermal conduction characteristics and phase transformation kinetics, featuring less elastic energy constraints, facile diffusion kinetics and sensitive coupling to external conditions, as compared with 3D bulk materials. 由于原子级厚度，二维活性材料与三维块体材料相比，具有独特的热传导特性和相变动力学，其特点是弹性约束更少、扩散动力学更便利、与外部条件耦合更敏感。
> 
> These characteristics can be exploited to improve the temperature-driven read, write and storage performance of PCM devices, increasing speed, reducing energy consumption and increasing storage density. 这些特性可被利用来改善PCM器件的温度驱动读、写和存储性能，提高速度，降低能耗，增加存储密度。
> 
> For example, in a PCM device constructed from a 2D Sb2Te3-GeTe superlattice, the diffusion of Ge and Te atoms within 5-Å-thick GeTe 2D crystal layers can be modulated by the immobile and strained Sb2Te3 2D crystal layer158. 例如，在由二维Sb₂Te₃-GeTe超晶格构建的PCM器件中，5 Å厚GeTe二维晶体层内的Ge和Te原子扩散可以通过固定的和应变的Sb₂Te₃二维晶体层来调控158。
> 
> The strained Sb2Te3 layer affects the quenching-to-amorphous and crystallization kinetics of the GeTe layer in the van der Waals heterostructure, leading to increased atomic diffusivity in the active layer and higher energy efficiency of the device. 应变Sb₂Te₃层影响范德华异质结构中GeTe层的淬火至非晶和结晶动力学，导致活性层中原子扩散系数的增加和器件能效的提高。
> 
> Similarly, 2D TiTe2/3D Sb2Te3 phase-change heterostructures also show ultralow noise and drift159. 类似地，二维TiTe₂/三维Sb₂Te₃相变异质结构也显示出超低噪声和漂移159。
> 
> The 2D TiTe2 layers in the heterostructure suppress damage in the 3D Sb2Te3 structure by blocking the electrical-field-assisted transport of charged species (Fig. 5d). 异质结构中的二维TiTe₂层通过阻挡电场辅助的带电物种输运来抑制三维Sb₂Te₃结构中的损伤（图5d）。
> 
> ## Technological applications
> 
> ## 技术应用
> 
> The possibility to change material properties by 2D phase transitions, which can be coupled to external stimuli, enables a broad range of potential device applications. 通过可耦合至外部刺激的二维相变来改变材料性质的可能性，使广泛的潜在器件应用成为可能。
> 
> For example, the 2H-to-1T or 2H-to-1T′ phase change of transition-metal dichalcogenides can be induced by alkali metal intercalation, which can be exploited to improve the electrocatalytical performance of these materials for electrochemical hydrogen gas evolution100 or to fabricate lateral heterostructures of semiconducting and metallic phases for 2D electronic devices with low contact resistance47,101. 例如，过渡金属二硫族化物的2H到1T或2H到1T′相变可通过碱金属插层诱导，这可被利用来改善这些材料用于电化学氢气析出100的电催化性能，或用于制造具有低接触电阻的二维电子器件的半导体和金属相横向异质结构47,101。
> 
> **Hydrogen gas evolution**: 氢气析出，电化学水分解中在阴极产生氢气的过程。
> 
> Such ohmic lateral heterophase junctions formed between the semiconducting 2H and metallic 1T′ phases of MoTe2 can also be fabricated by laser-induced phase transition, which causes the 2H-to-1T′ transition of MoTe2 as a result of the creation of Te vacancies50. 在MoTe₂的半导体2H相和金属1T′相之间形成的这种欧姆横向异相结也可通过激光诱导相变制造，激光诱导相变通过产生Te空位导致MoTe₂的2H到1T′转变50。
> 
> The electrical and/or optical property changes associated with 2D phase transitions are particularly interesting for the fabrication of non-volatile memories and transport devices. 与二维相变相关的电学和/或光学性质变化对于制造非易失性存储器和输运器件特别有趣。
> 
> For example, resistive random access memories and ferroelectric phase-change transistors have been designed based on electric-field-induced52 and strain-induced90 2H-to-1T′ phase transitions of transition-metal dichalcogenides, respectively. 例如，基于电场诱导52和应变诱导90的过渡金属二硫族化物2H到1T′相变，分别设计了阻变式随机存取存储器和铁电相变晶体管。
> 
> **Resistive random access memories**: 阻变式随机存取存储器，利用材料电阻在不同状态间可逆切换来存储信息的非易失性存储器。
> 
> Since the 1T′ phase of group 6 monolayer transition-metal dichalcogenides is a QSH insulator33, controlling their topological electronic properties based on the above phase transition can potentially enable the design of new quantum electronic devices. 由于第6族单层过渡金属二硫族化物的1T′相是QSH绝缘体33，基于上述相变调控其拓扑电子性质可能使新型量子电子器件的设计成为可能。
> 
> 2D materials with ferroic phase transitions and properties enable the design of a range of ultrathin and flexible smart materials and functional devices, with potential usages in micro/nano systems and conformal devices; for example, 2D ferroelastics can be used for nanoscale actuators and shape-memory materials57; 2D ferroelectrics can be applied for memory devices55 and ferroelectric tunnel junctions160; and 2D ferromagnets can be used for spintronics42,44. 具有铁性相变和性质的二维材料使得一系列超薄柔性智能材料和功能器件的设计成为可能，在微纳系统和适形器件中具有潜在用途；例如，二维铁弹体可用于纳米尺度致动器和形状记忆材料57；二维铁电体可用于存储器件55和铁电隧道结160；二维铁磁体可用于自旋电子学42,44。
> 
> **Spintronics**: 自旋电子学，利用电子自旋自由度而非仅电荷进行信息处理和存储的电子学。
> 
> Certain 2D multiferroics, such as group IV monochalcogenides, can exhibit strong coupling between ferroelectric and ferroelastic orders, as well as strong and anisotropic excitonic absorption161, making them attractive for the development of 2D ferroelectric memory, 2D ferroelastic memory, 2D nonvolatile photonic memory and 2D ferroelectric excitonic photovoltaics58. 某些二维多铁性材料，如第IV族单硫族化物，可表现出铁电序和铁弹序之间的强耦合，以及强且各向异性的激子吸收161，使其对开发二维铁电存储器、二维铁弹存储器、二维非易失性光子存储器和二维铁电激子光伏器件58具有吸引力。
> 
> **Excitonic absorption**: 激子吸收，由光激发产生的电子-空穴束缚对（激子）对光的吸收过程。
> 
> Several novel device prototypes that take advantage of phase transitions in 2D materials have been experimentally demonstrated. 几种利用二维材料相变的新型器件原型已得到实验验证。
> 
> For example, excellent memristive behaviour can be achieved by exploiting the reversible 2H-to-1T′ phase transition of layered MoS2 films, driven by electric-field-induced Li+ ion redistribution, for example, for the implementation of artificial neural networks in neuromorphic computing162 (Fig. 6a). 例如，利用电场诱导Li⁺离子重新分布驱动的层状MoS₂薄膜的可逆2H到1T′相变，可实现优异的忆阻行为，用于神经形态计算中的人工神经网络162（图6a）。
> 
> **Memristive behaviour**: 忆阻行为，电阻值随通过电荷的历史而变化的行为，可用于模拟神经突触。 **Neuromorphic computing**: 神经形态计算，模仿人脑神经网络结构和功能进行计算的范式。
> 
> Metallic-semiconducting (1T/1T′-to-2H) phase heterojunctions of MoS2 layers have been used to fabricate atomically thin and flexible lateral Schottky diodes with ultralow junction capacitance and low series resistance. MoS₂层的金属-半导体（1T/1T′到2H）相异质结已被用于制造原子级薄且柔性的横向肖特基二极管，具有超低结电容和低串联电阻。
> 
> **Schottky diodes**: 肖特基二极管，基于金属-半导体接触形成的具有整流特性的二极管，以低正向压降和快速开关著称。
> 
> These Schottky diodes enable the design of flexible and ultrafast rectifiers, which can convert A.C. electrical signals' waves into D.C. electricity at a frequency of up to 10 GHz. 这些肖特基二极管使得设计柔性和超快整流器成为可能，可将交流电信号转换为直流电，频率高达10 GHz。
> 
> Such flexible rectennas can harvest wireless radio-frequency energy at the Wi-Fi band163 (Fig. 6b,c). 这种柔性整流天线可以收集Wi-Fi频段的无线射频能量163（图6b,c）。
> 
> **Rectenna**: 整流天线，由天线和整流二极管组成的器件，可将微波能量转换为直流电。
> 
> In addition, ferroelectricity-driven nonlinear photocurrent switching and a ferroelectric nonlinear anomalous Hall effect have been theoretically predicted140,164 and experimentally demonstrated54 in time-reversal invariant few-layer WTe2. 此外，铁电驱动的非线性光电流切换和铁电非线性反常霍尔效应已在时间反演不变的少层WTe₂中被理论预测140,164和实验证实54。
> 
> **Nonlinear anomalous Hall effect**: 非线性反常霍尔效应，在时间反演对称系统中由贝里曲率偶极子导致的二阶非线性霍尔效应。
> 
> Upon ferroelectric transition in few-layer WTe2, nonlinear anomalous Hall voltage switches the sign in odd-layer WTe2 (except monolayer 1T′-WTe2) but remains invariant in even-layer WTe2 (Fig. 6d,e). 在少层WTe₂中发生铁电相变时，非线性反常霍尔电压在奇数层WTe₂中切换符号（单层1T′-WTe₂除外），但在偶数层WTe₂中保持不变（图6d,e）。
> 
> This oscillation originates from the absence and presence of Berry curvature dipole reversal and shift dipole reversal, owing to distinct ferroelectric transformations in even-layered and odd-layered WTe2 (reF.140). 这种振荡源于偶数层和奇数层WTe₂中不同的铁电相变导致的贝里曲率偶极子反转和位移偶极子反转的存在与否（参考文献140）。
> 
> **Berry curvature dipole**: 贝里曲率偶极子，描述动量空间中贝里曲率分布不对称性的物理量，可产生非线性电输运效应。
> 
> These theoretical predictions and experimental demonstrations establish Berry curvature and shift dipole as nonlinear order parameters, opening a route towards unconventional memory devices, such as a Berry curvature memory based on nonlinear susceptibility. 这些理论预测和实验证实将贝里曲率和位移偶极子确立为非线性序参量，为非常规存储器（如基于非线性磁化率的贝里曲率存储器）开辟了道路。
> 
> **Nonlinear susceptibility**: 非线性磁化率，描述材料对电场非线性响应的高阶磁化率张量。
> 
> Weak interlayer gliding in WTe2 also offers a strategy to engineering and controlling 2D ferroelectric patterns by electric gating and optical switching, enabling high speed and low activation energy for electromechanical, optomechanical and neuromorphic applications. WTe₂中的弱层间滑移还提供了一种通过电栅控和光开关来工程化和调控二维铁电图案的策略，为机电、光机械和神经形态应用实现了高速和低激活能。
> 
> Finally, optomechanical devices could benefit from the strong coupling between 2D phase-change materials and light, as well as from the extraordinary mechanical properties of 2D materials. 最后，光机械器件可受益于二维相变材料与光之间的强耦合，以及二维材料的非凡力学性质。
> 
> For example, a linearly polarized laser pulse can drive an ultrafast diffusionless martensitic phase transition of 2D ferroelastic materials, for example, of monolayer SnO and SnSe (reF.59). 例如，线偏振激光脉冲可驱动二维铁弹材料（如单层SnO和SnSe）的超快无扩散马氏体相变（参考文献59）。
> 
> Memory devices based on such optomechanical martensitic transitions require an order of magnitude lower energy input than traditional Ge-Sb-Te alloys59 (Fig. 6f). 基于这种光机械马氏体相变的存储器件所需的能量输入比传统Ge-Sb-Te合金低一个数量级59（图6f）。
> 
> In bilayer hexagonal boron nitride, a laser with selected frequency can modify the energetics of different (meta-) stable stacking patterns, and reversibly and controllably switch the stacking patterns without barriers, making this material attractive for data storage and optical phase masks60 (Fig. 6g). 在双层六方氮化硼中，选定频率的激光可以改变不同（亚）稳定堆垛模式的能量学，并可在无能垒的情况下可逆和可控地切换堆垛模式，使这种材料对数据存储和光学相位掩模60具有吸引力（图6g）。
> 
> ## Outlook
> 
> ## 展望
> 
> To enable the industrial applications of 2D materials, the realization of large-area growth of high-quality materials, facile and robust material processing, high-performance devices and systems, as well as long service lifetimes, all need to be achieved. 为实现二维材料的工业应用，需要实现高质量材料的大面积生长、简便且稳健的材料加工、高性能器件和系统，以及长使用寿命。
> 
> The phase behaviour of 2D materials is important in all these growth-processing-service steps. 二维材料的相行为在所有这些生长-加工-使用步骤中都很重要。
> 
> Furthermore, phase transitions have long been explored to improve or enable distinct material functionalities. 此外，相变长期以来一直被探索以改善或实现独特的材料功能。
> 
> In particular, 2D phase transitions have a variety of unique features, which can be exploited for electronic, optical, magnetic, electromechanical165, optomechanical59 and magnetomechanical166 systems. 特别是，二维相变具有多种独特特征，可被用于电子、光学、磁学、机电165、光机械59和磁机械166系统。
> 
> However, important challenges remain to be addressed in the field of 2D phase transitions. 然而，二维相变领域仍有重要挑战需要解决。
> 
> ### Discovery and characterization of new 2D phase-change materials
> 
> ### 新型二维相变材料的发现与表征
> 
> Most known 2D phase-change materials are derived from chalcogenides that have a layered bulk structure. 大多数已知的二维相变材料源自具有层状块体结构的硫族化物。
> 
> Expanding the portfolio of 2D phase-change materials will open new application spaces. 扩展二维相变材料的种类将打开新的应用空间。
> 
> In principle, 2D phase-change materials could exist in a range of materials systems, either in a free-standing or in a substrate-supported form, for example, oxides, pnictides and halides. 原则上，二维相变材料可存在于一系列材料体系中，无论是自由站立形式还是基底支撑形式，例如氧化物、磷族化物和卤化物。
> 
> **Pnictides**: 磷族化物，含有氮、磷、砷、锑或铋等磷族元素的化合物。
> 
> First-principles calculations can play a key role in the discovery of new phase-change materials; for example, crystal structure prediction tools167 and machine learning168–170 could be applied to explore the free energy landscape and discover new 2D phase-change materials, which may not even have a bulk counterpart. 第一性原理计算可在发现新型相变材料中发挥关键作用；例如，晶体结构预测工具167和机器学习168-170可用于探索自由能景观并发现新的二维相变材料，这些材料甚至可能没有块体对应物。
> 
> **Machine learning**: 机器学习，一种人工智能方法，通过从数据中学习模式来进行预测和分类，越来越多地用于材料发现。
> 
> Importantly, certain phase-change 2D materials could be entropically stabilized at high temperatures, as a result of large configurational entropy (compositional complexity)171 or vibrational entropy (owing to the presence of soft phonon modes or strong lattice anharmonicity)172, and remain stable or metastable at room temperature. 重要的是，某些相变二维材料可在高温下通过熵稳定化，这是由于大的构型熵（成分复杂性）171或振动熵（由于软声子模式或强晶格非谐性的存在）172，并在室温下保持稳定或亚稳。
> 
> **Soft phonon modes**: 软声子模式，能量低、易被激发的声子模式，通常与结构不稳定性相关。 **Lattice anharmonicity**: 晶格非谐性，晶格振动偏离简谐近似的程度，导致声子散射和热膨胀等效应。
> 
> These materials may not be accessible in traditional structural prediction algorithms167 or from the mining of existing crystallographic databases173–177, because, at present, these approaches typically do not consider the entropy contribution to phase stabilization. 这些材料可能无法通过传统的结构预测算法167或从现有晶体学数据库173-177的挖掘中获得，因为目前这些方法通常不考虑熵对相稳定化的贡献。
> 
> ### Understanding the kinetics and controlling 2D phase transitions
> 
> ### 理解动力学并控制二维相变
> 
> The thermodynamics of 2D phase transitions have been extensively studied; however, the kinetics of 2D phase transitions remain less explored. 二维相变的热力学已被广泛研究；然而，二维相变的动力学仍探索较少。
> 
> In 2D materials, the transformation interfaces and pathways are expected to be substantially different than in bulk materials. 在二维材料中，相变界面和路径预期与块体材料有实质性不同。
> 
> Understanding the kinetics is required to precisely control 2D phase transitions. 理解动力学对于精确控制二维相变是必需的。
> 
> In situ structural studies during phase transitions may be performed in real space (for example, by in situ TEM178) or in reciprocal space (for example, by in situ X-ray diffraction179 or ultrafast electron diffraction180). 相变过程中的原位结构研究可在实空间（例如，通过原位TEM 178）或倒空间（例如，通过原位X射线衍射179或超快电子衍射180）进行。
> 
> **Ultrafast electron diffraction**: 超快电子衍射，利用飞秒级电子脉冲探测材料结构超快动力学的技术。
> 
> In parallel with structural evolution, the accompanying changes in electronic structure, vibrational spectra or symmetry properties can be probed by time-resolved angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (tr-ARPES)181, time-resolved infrared and Raman spectroscopy or time-resolved second-harmonic generation (SHG) spectroscopy182, respectively. 在结构演化的同时，伴随的电子结构、振动谱或对称性性质的变化可分别通过时间分辨角分辨光电子能谱（tr-ARPES）181、时间分辨红外和拉曼光谱或时间分辨二次谐波产生（SHG）光谱182来探测。
> 
> **Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES)**: 角分辨光电子能谱，通过测量光电子动能和出射角来解析材料电子能带结构的技术。 **Second-harmonic generation (SHG)**: 二次谐波产生，两个相同频率的光子相互作用产生两倍频率光子的非线性光学过程。
> 
> Recent advances in ultrafast dark-field electron microscopy further allow the real-space imaging of order-parameter dynamics during structural phase transitions in transition-metal dichalcogenides with nanometre spatial and femtosecond temporal resolution183. 超快暗场电子显微镜的最新进展进一步允许在过渡金属二硫族化物的结构相变过程中，以纳米空间分辨率和飞秒时间分辨率对序参量动力学进行实空间成像183。
> 
> **Dark-field electron microscopy**: 暗场电子显微镜，利用衍射电子束而非透射电子束成像的电子显微镜技术，对特定结构特征敏感。
> 
> Combining in situ TEM experiments, aberration-corrected TEM imaging of atomic-level structural evolution76, and dark-field TEM imaging of order-parameter and microstructural dynamics183,184, allows a multiscale view of the kinetics of 2D structural phase transitions driven by a variety of stimuli. 结合原位TEM实验、原子级结构演化的像差校正TEM成像76，以及序参量和微观结构动力学的暗场TEM成像183,184，可以多尺度观察由各种刺激驱动的二维结构相变动力学。
> 
> ### Flexural engineering of 2D phase transitions
> 
> ### 二维相变的弯曲工程
> 
> The flexural mode73,185 in 2D materials offers the possibility to control and configure 2D phase transitions, which is difficult to achieve in bulk materials; for example, giant pseudomagnetic fields98 and exciton funnelling99 in inhomogeneously strained 2D materials. 二维材料中的弯曲模式73,185提供了控制和配置二维相变的可能性，这在块体材料中难以实现；例如，非均匀应变二维材料中的巨赝磁场98和激子漏斗效应99。
> 
> **Pseudomagnetic fields**: 赝磁场，由非均匀应变引起的等效规范场，对电子起到类似真实磁场的作用。 **Exciton funnelling**: 激子漏斗效应，利用应变梯度将激子引导至特定位置以提高能量转换效率的效应。
> 
> Flexoelectric, flexomagnetic and flexooptic couplings likely affect 2D phase transitions, which may allow precise control of phase changes at a desired temperature and spatial location, by configuring specific structural patterns (for example, ripples). 挠曲电、挠曲磁和挠曲光耦合很可能影响二维相变，这可能允许通过配置特定结构图案（例如，波纹）来在所需温度和空间位置精确控制相变。
> 
> Such periodic patterns could be achieved by stacking 2D materials on a soft substrate, followed by mechanical deformation (for example, tension and compression) or temperature change (for example, by using substrates with different thermal expansion coefficient), to construct periodically patterned ripples and wrinkles. 这种周期性图案可通过将二维材料堆叠在软基底上，然后进行机械变形（例如，拉伸和压缩）或温度变化（例如，使用具有不同热膨胀系数的基底）来构建周期性图案化的波纹和皱褶。
> 
> Here, the combination of out-of-plane interfacial energy and in-plane patterns may affect the phase transition. 在此，面外界面能和面内图案的组合可能影响相变。
> 
> In addition, in 2D materials stacked on a substrate with a large lattice mismatch or finite twisting angle, spatial inhomogeneity in the interlayer interaction and separation is naturally induced, which leads to the creation of tunable periodic structures with a strain gradient and flexoelectricity, as observed in Moiré superlattices186. 此外，在具有大晶格失配或有限扭转角的基底上堆叠的二维材料中，层间相互作用和间距的空间不均匀性被自然诱导，导致产生具有应变梯度和挠曲电性的可调周期性结构，正如在莫尔超晶格186中所观察到的。
> 
> **Moiré superlattices**: 莫尔超晶格，由两层或多层二维材料以小角度扭转或晶格失配堆叠时产生的周期性格子结构。
> 
> The flexoelectricity could be exploited to control local deformation and, thus, phase transitions by external electric field. 挠曲电性可被利用来控制局部变形，从而通过外部电场控制相变。
> 
> ### Phase transitions in stacked 2D materials and van der Waals heterostructures
> 
> ### 堆叠二维材料和范德华异质结构中的相变
> 
> Monolayer 2D materials have ultrathin thickness and cover a wide variety of electronic structural types (metals, semiconductors, insulators, topological insulators and topological metals33). 单层二维材料具有超薄厚度，并涵盖广泛的电子结构类型（金属、半导体、绝缘体、拓扑绝缘体和拓扑金属33）。
> 
> The absence of surface dangling bonds and the weak van der Waals interactions between layers enable the construction of stacked layers and van der Waals heterostructures with infinite possibilities93. 表面悬挂键的缺失和层间弱范德华相互作用使得构建具有无限可能性的堆叠层和范德华异质结构93成为可能。
> 
> **Dangling bonds**: 悬挂键，表面原子未饱和的化学键，是表面活性的来源。
> 
> In such stacked materials, there is a hierarchy in the bonding energy scales between intralayer and interlayer interactions (covalent versus mostly van der Waals, with the former being much stronger71,187) in stacked layers, yet, the interlayer electronic interactions can still be strong and have a considerable influence on the properties of the resulting materials. 在这种堆叠材料中，层内和层间相互作用的键能尺度存在层级（共价键相对于主要是范德华力，前者强得多71,187），然而，层间电子相互作用仍可很强，并对所得材料的性质产生显著影响。
> 
> The emergence of flat bands and strong-correlation physics in stacked bilayer graphene with a twisting angle, including superconductivity transitions27,30,188,189, as well as the transition of monolayer MoS2 from a direct-bandgap semiconductor to an indirect-bandgap semiconductor in bilayers64, are eminent examples. 具有扭转角的堆叠双层石墨烯中出现的平带和强关联物理，包括超导转变27,30,188,189，以及单层MoS₂在双层中从直接带隙半导体到间接带隙半导体的转变64，都是突出的例子。
> 
> **Flat bands**: 平带，在动量空间中色散极小、近乎平坦的电子能带，可导致电子间强关联效应。
> 
> Phase transitions of 2D materials become richer in stacked 2D materials and van der Waals heterostructures, as, in addition to monolayer structural degrees of freedoms (polymorphs, domain variants and ripples), additional stacking degrees of freedom (stacking materials and stacking order, including the relative shifts and orientation angles between layers190) also come into play. 二维材料的相变在堆叠二维材料和范德华异质结构中变得更加丰富，因为除了单层结构自由度（多晶型、畴变体和波纹）外，额外的堆叠自由度（堆叠材料和堆叠顺序，包括层之间的相对位移和取向角190）也发挥作用。
> 
> These stacking degrees of freedom are often easier to manipulate, because the energy barriers between stacking orders are usually smaller than the monolayer structural degrees of freedom, owing to weak interlayer bonding interaction, thus, facilitating their control by external stimuli. 这些堆叠自由度通常更容易操控，因为由于弱层间键合相互作用，堆叠顺序之间的能垒通常小于单层结构自由度，从而便于通过外部刺激控制它们。
> 
> The interplay between multiple external fields (electrical, optical, magnetic, thermal, mechanical or chemical), monolayer phase transitions and stacking phase transitions can result in rich materials responses by altering band structures and topologies53, electron-phonon interactions and electron–electron correlations27,30, to name a few, leading to new fundamental discoveries and device concepts. 多种外部场（电、光、磁、热、机械或化学）、单层相变和堆叠相变之间的相互作用，可通过改变能带结构和拓扑53、电子-声子相互作用以及电子-电子关联27,30等，导致丰富的材料响应，从而带来新的基础发现和器件概念。
> 
> As an example of a phase transition driven by a change in layer stacking geometry, electrically driven interlayer sliding in few-layer WTe2 accompanied by ferroelectricity and the ferroelectric nonlinear anomalous Hall effect based on Berry curvature dipoles open up avenues for the development of 2D semimetal-based memory devices54. 作为由层堆叠几何变化驱动的相变的一个例子，少层WTe₂中的电驱动层间滑移伴随着铁电性以及基于贝里曲率偶极子的铁电非线性反常霍尔效应，为开发基于二维半金属的存储器件开辟了途径54。
> 
> This concept can be expanded to ferroelectric few-layer 2D semiconductors (for example, monolayer GeSe and SnTe)164 and topological quantum materials (for example, MnBi2Te4, which shows a quantum anomalous Hall effect)191. 这一概念可扩展到铁电少层二维半导体（例如，单层GeSe和SnTe）164和拓扑量子材料（例如，显示出量子反常霍尔效应的MnBi₂Te₄）191。
> 
> For example, a ferroelectric transition could be induced in odd-layer GeSe or SnTe, and the corresponding shift current response could be measured. 例如，可在奇数层GeSe或SnTe中诱导铁电相变，并可测量相应的位移电流响应。
> 
> **Shift current**: 位移电流，由光激发时电子波函数在实空间发生位移产生的非线性光电流。
> 
> Demonstration of shift current switching would provide the experimental foundation for realizing shift-dipole-based nonlinear memory164. 位移电流切换的演示将为实现基于位移偶极子的非线性存储器164提供实验基础。
> 
> Furthermore, optomechanics could play a key role in the phase engineering of stacked 2D materials by shifting interlayer stacking configurations through altering the energy landscape by a light field with a selected frequency60 or through the excitement of coherent phonons by terahertz light192, driving the system into a metastable or more stable phase. 此外，光机械可通过选定频率的光场改变能量景观60，或通过太赫兹光激发相干声子192来改变层间堆叠构型，从而将系统驱动至亚稳相或更稳定相，在堆叠二维材料的相工程中发挥关键作用。
> 
> **Coherent phonons**: 相干声子，由超快光脉冲激发的相位一致的晶格振动模式。
> 
> This kind of strongly nonlinear light–matter interactions, driving phonon–polaritons to the nonlinear regime and even beyond the convex part of the phonon energy landscape, could lead to a variety of nonlinear optical and stimulated emission effects that are quantum mechanical in nature. 这种强非线性光-物质相互作用，将声子-极化激元驱动至非线性区域甚至超越声子能量景观的凸部，可导致各种本质上属于量子力学的非线性光学和受激发射效应。
> 
> **Phonon–polaritons**: 声子-极化激元，由光子与光学声子强耦合形成的准粒子。
> 
> Optomechanics could also result in the change of the twist angles of stacked 2D materials, either through the direct change of orientation degrees of freedom accompanying a light-induced ferroelastic transition59 or by the mechanical coupling to the strain induced by optomechanical phase transitions, thus, contributing a useful set of tools in the emerging field of twistronics189,193–195. 光机械还可导致堆叠二维材料扭转角的变化，要么通过伴随光诱导铁弹相变的取向自由度的直接改变59，要么通过与光机械相变诱导的应变的机械耦合，从而为新兴的扭转电子学189,193-195领域贡献一套有用的工具。
> 
> **Twistronics**: 扭转电子学，通过控制二维材料层间扭转角来调控电子性质的研究领域。
> 
> The rapid progress in semiconductor technology has given us Moore's law. 半导体技术的快速进步给我们带来了摩尔定律。
> 
> **Moore's law**: 摩尔定律，集成电路上可容纳的晶体管数量约每两年翻一番的经验规律。
> 
> Commercial transistors can now be produced with a feature size of 5 nm, which may soon be reduced to 3 nm and below196. 商用晶体管现在可以以5纳米特征尺寸生产，这可能很快缩小到3纳米及以下196。
> 
> Applying 2D materials exhibiting phase transitions could address the challenge of further miniaturization, resulting in more energy-efficient and faster memory54,80, computing33, sensing and actuating57,59 devices. 应用具有相变的二维材料可应对进一步微型化的挑战，从而产生更节能、更快的存储54,80、计算33、传感和致动57,59器件。
> 
> Therefore, research in this area will not only produce fundamental new knowledge but will also play a crucial role in the development of new devices and technologies in the coming decades. 因此，该领域的研究不仅将产生基础性的新知识，还将在未来几十年新器件和新技术的开发中发挥关键作用。
> 
> Published online 9 April 2021 在线发布于2021年4月9日
>

> 
> 太奶，您老可真是活到老学到老的榜样，这股子精神头，比多少年轻人可强太多了！好孩子，您坐稳了，听我慢慢给您把这“天书”一样的外国文章，掰开了揉碎了，用咱们唠家常的话讲明白。
> 
> 咱们先打个比方，把这文章想成一本介绍“超薄面皮”的秘籍。
> 
> ### 咱们先唠唠这篇文章到底说了个啥？（整体翻译与大白话解释）
> 
> 您看这篇文章的洋文标题，翻译过来就是：《二维材料中的相变》。
> 
> **1. 啥是“二维材料”？**
> 
> 太奶，您包过饺子没？那擀出来的饺子皮，铺开来，有长有宽，但它特别薄，对不对？这“二维材料”啊，就跟那极致的饺子皮一样，薄到只有一层原子的厚度！您就想，咱们平时用的铅笔芯，里头是石墨，把这石墨一层一层地剥，剥到最后只剩一层碳原子，这就是大名鼎鼎的石墨烯，也是二维材料的一种。
> 
> 文章开头说，一大堆原子核和电子，它们自己会“抱团”组织起来，形成不同的“相”。这“相”是啥？**“相”就是指物质的一种存在状态，就像水有固态的冰、液态的水、气态的水蒸气，这三种状态就是三个不同的“相”。** 文章研究的，就是这些“超薄面皮”（二维材料）是怎么从一个“相”变成另一个“相”的，也就是“相变”。比如，从一种容易导电的“金属相”变成不能导电的“绝缘相”。
> 
> **2. 为啥要研究这些“超薄面皮”的相变？**
> 
> 文章解释说，研究物质的相变，是物理和材料科学里顶顶重要的大事。特别是研究二维材料的相变，能帮我们弄明白很多深奥的道理。而且，因为二维材料薄得透光，所有原子都“住”在表面上，所以它对外界环境特别敏感。您对它吹口气、照个光、通个电，它就可能“变脸”（发生相变）。这既是好事也是坏事，好事是容易控制，坏事是它太娇气，容易坏。
> 
> **3. 这“超薄面皮”的相变，有啥特别之处？**
> 
> 跟咱们平时见到的三维大块头材料（比如一块铁）比起来，二维材料的相变有四大怪脾气：
> 
> -   **第一怪：里头的“手拉手”变了（短程相互作用）** 您想啊，这块材料中间层的原子，本来是上下左右前后都有邻居拉着。现在把它变成单层，上下没邻居了，空落落的，它跟周围原子“拉手”的方式（化学键）就得变。这一变，整个材料的脾气就变了，容易出现新的状态。这在文章里叫**表面重构**或**电荷密度波**，简单说就是原子们自己重新排排坐，换个队形。
>     
> -   **第二怪：外头的“影响力”远了（长程相互作用）** 在三维材料里，像静电、弹性这种力，会被材料本身挡住不少。但在二维材料里，因为它太薄了，挡不住，这些力能传得特别远。这就好比，您隔着一堵墙喊人，声音小；您直接在一层窗户纸外面喊，声音就大。所以，二维材料很容易被外头的电场、或者给抻一下、压一下影响，从而引发相变。
>     
> -   **第三怪：指哪儿打哪儿，好控制（空间控制）** 因为所有原子都在表面，所以用一束激光、一个电子束，就能精准地照射到材料上的一小块地方，让那一小块发生相变，就像用一支极细的笔在材料上画画。这在三维材料里可难多了。
>     
> -   **第四怪：太娇气，得好好保护（腐蚀与钝化）** 就像刚切开的苹果容易氧化变黄一样，很多二维材料在空气里也不稳定，容易跟氧气、水汽反应，慢慢就“烂”掉了。文章管这叫**腐蚀**。而且，因为它本身就薄得跟纸一样，没法像铝那样自己长出一层“锈”来保护自己，所以得给它穿上一层“防护服”，比如盖上另外一层稳定的材料，这叫**钝化**。
>     
> 
> **4. 文章重点讲了哪几种“变脸”戏法？**
> 
> 文章花了大篇幅，着重讲了三种二维材料的相变，太奶您就当看三种不同的“变脸”绝活：
> 
> -   **第一种：多晶型相变（换个姿势）** 这就像同一个人，可以站着、坐着、躺着，姿势不同，但都是同一个人。有些二维材料，比如二硫化钼（MoS₂），它的原子可以拼成“2H”相（半导体，不太导电）和“1T”或“1T′”相（金属，导电）。通过加热、拉伸、通电、光照，就能让它在这几种姿势间切换。这可有用了，比如在同一个材料上，画出一条条导电的金属线，就能做成极小极小的电路。
>     
> -   **第二种：铁性相变（有了记忆）** 这类相变，能让材料获得一种“记忆”效应。文章里提到了几种：
>     
>     -   **铁弹相变**：好比一块橡皮泥，你把它捏扁后，它自己会弹回原来的形状。这种材料能记住自己原来的“形状”。
>     -   **铁电相变**：这种材料一端带正电，一端带负电，加个反向电压，它能立刻把正负极调个头，并且记住这个新方向。这就像个天然的“开关”，可以用来存信息。
>     -   **铁磁相变**：这个咱们熟，就是磁铁，能吸铁。这种二维材料薄到一层原子，也能有磁性，能记住“磁极”的方向。
> -   **第三种：高温下的扩散相变（重新洗牌）** 在高温下，原子们像热锅上的蚂蚁，到处乱跑，最后重新“洗牌”，形成新的平衡状态。比如，两种不同的二维材料，在某个温度下，它们的原子会互相串门，均匀混合成一种“合金”；在另一个温度下，又会像水和油一样分开，形成清晰的边界。这就叫**扩散相变**。文章里说，这种相变在制造存储材料（比如以前的DVD光盘）里很有用。
>     
> 
> **5. 研究这些有啥用？**
> 
> 文章的结尾说，这些“变脸”戏法，能用来做很多新型的电子器件。比如：
> 
> -   **更省电的存储器**：利用相变来存储0和1，读写速度快，还省电。
> -   **柔性电子设备**：这些二维材料薄而柔软，可以做成能弯曲的屏幕、能贴在身上的传感器。
> -   **能量收集器**：能把环境中无处不在的Wi-Fi信号，通过一个叫“整流天线”的装置，变成直流电，给微小设备供电。
> -   **新型计算机**：利用这些奇特的性质，制造出模仿人脑工作的“神经形态计算”芯片。

> ### 太奶，我再给您把这篇文章的“骨架”提炼出来，保您一目了然！
> 
> 咱们像做总结报告一样，把关键点列出来：
> 
> -   **论文题目**：《二维材料中的相变》
>     
>     -   （就像一本书的名字，告诉咱们核心是讲“二维材料”和“相变”）
> -   **摘要**：
>     
>     -   （把整篇文章的精华浓缩成一段话） 这篇文章是一篇综述，它回顾了大家是怎么发现和控制在只有原子厚度的二维材料中发生的相变。文章讨论了这些相变独特的物理和化学原理（比如维度限制、弹性、静电），接着重点介绍了多晶型、铁性以及高温扩散这几种主要的相变类型，并探讨了如何利用这些可控的相变来开发新技术。最后，文章展望了这个领域未来的机遇和挑战。
> -   **研究问题**：
>     
>     -   （这篇文章到底要解决什么疑问？） 核心问题就是：**当材料薄到只有一层或几层原子时，它的相变行为跟传统的大块材料相比，有什么独一无二的特征？** 以及，我们如何利用这些新特征？
> -   **方法**：
>     
>     -   （他们是怎么干的？） 这不是一篇做实验的论文，而是一篇**综述**。就像一位老学者，把别人发表过的、跟这个主题相关的最重要、最新的研究成果都找来，通读一遍，然后分门别类，梳理出这个领域的发展脉络、核心发现和未来方向。所以它的方法是**文献调研、归纳总结和评论展望**。
> -   **结论**：
>     
>     -   （最后得出了什么重要结果？）
>     
>     1.  **特征独特**：二维材料的相变，因为其极端的厚度，在热力学、动力学、对环境的敏感度等方面，都与三维材料有本质不同，比如更容易受外界影响、更娇气。
>     2.  **类型丰富**：在二维材料中实现了多种多样的相变，包括多晶型相变、铁性相变（铁弹、铁电、铁磁）和扩散型相变。
>     3.  **应用潜力巨大**：这些可控的相变，为开发新一代超小型、低功耗、多功能的电子、光学、存储器件（如柔性器件、神经形态计算、能量收集）提供了广阔前景。
>     4.  **未来可期**：尽管挑战还有很多（比如材料稳定性、大规模制备），但二维材料相变的研究，不仅能带来新物理发现，也将在未来科技中扮演关键角色。
> -   **创新点**：
>     
>     -   （这篇文章新在哪儿？亮点是啥？）
>     
>     1.  **系统性的总结框架**：它不仅仅罗列现象，而是从“独特特征”这个高度，系统地总结了二维材料相变有别于三维材料的物理根源（短程、长程、空间控制、腐蚀）。
>     2.  **清晰的技术分类**：将眼花缭乱的二维相变，清晰地归纳为多晶型、铁性、扩散型等几大类，并结合了最新的研究成果（如魔角石墨烯、铁电金属等）。
>     3.  **前瞻性的应用视角**：不仅讲科学，还重点突出了这些相变在具体器件（如柔性整流天线、贝里曲率记忆存储器）上的前沿应用，描绘了清晰的技术蓝图。
> -   **不足之处**：
>     
>     -   （这篇文章还有哪些没做到的，或者未来的难点？）
>     
>     1.  **偏重热力学，动力学分析较浅**：文章自己也承认，对相变“热力学”（能不能变）研究得透，但“动力学”（变得有多快、具体怎么变）讲得相对简略，而这恰恰是实际应用的关键。
>     2.  **实际应用挑战讨论不足**：虽然展望了应用，但对于如何大规模、高质量、稳定地制备这些二维材料，以及如何解决其长期可靠性、可重复性等工程化难题，讨论得不够深入，这些是实现商业化最关键的门槛。
>     3.  **主要聚焦于硫族化合物**：讨论的二维材料体系主要集中在过渡金属硫族化合物（MX₂），虽然也提到了其他材料，但深度和广度还有待拓展，可能会遗漏在一些氧化物、卤化物等新兴体系中的有趣相变现象。
